### T-003 constraint — retained, not repaired away

`test_standalone_package_schema_and_evaluation` requires the temporary package
and every ancestor to have no `.git`. This sandbox exposes a read-only
`/tmp/.git`; even the task-owned TMPDIR has that ancestor. The exact assertion
fails before package evaluation. `T-003-environment.log` records the directory
and ancestor probe. The other 30 tests passed and the genuine Windows junction
case was skipped on Linux. The caller's earlier 31-pass run is prior evidence,
not a substitute for this run. Do not delete or alter the shared `/tmp/.git`
or weaken the standalone-package assertion. Root must rerun this suite in a
writable temporary location with no Git ancestor before considering that Done
When satisfied. No out-of-scope T-003 code was modified.

During finalization, the caller added `T-003-outer-host.log` to this directory.
It records a separate 32-case execution: 31 passed and the genuine Windows
junction case skipped. Its bytes are pinned in Outputs as caller-provided test
evidence, not as an execution by this sandbox session. It contains no command
or invocation identity header; root owns confirmation of its execution
provenance. Preserve both logs rather than replacing the failed local run.
Caller-owned live native-event/stderr launch streams are deliberately excluded
from this report's output hashes: they are still being written by the host,
not task implementation outputs, and root owns their final sealing.

### CI and platform limits

The Bash failure-propagating invocation and PowerShell Python loop register the
foundation suite, and the hosted workflow invokes it in the native
Windows/macOS/Linux matrix. The PowerShell fixture executes the extracted loop
with mock Python commands for preference, fallback, absence and failure; it is
not a hosted CI run. No full repository suite was run here; root owns that run.
Native Windows junction and Windows/macOS CI execution remain pending real CI.
No newly reachable environment-SKIP branch was introduced by these addenda.
The prior registration makes the foundation real-symlink branch reachable on
CI hosts with privileges; Windows execution is pending first real CI execution.

## Disposable rollback experiment

Created `/tmp/cloud-t001-01a14d52/rollback` using
`git clone --shared --quiet --no-hardlinks . /tmp/cloud-t001-01a14d52/rollback`
at the recorded base revision. No original worktree or shared fixture was reset.
`rollback-experiment.py` overlays the six task-owned candidate files from the
initial snapshot (including pre-existing uncommitted repair bytes), runs 53
baseline checks, deliberately replaces the inventory heading with a nonexistent
heading, and records the genuine nonzero rejection. It then restores **only**
those six files from the initial snapshot, verifies every before/restored hash
is identical, reruns 53 checks and all four PowerShell runner cases successfully.
`rollback.json` records exact commands, base revision and before/mutated/restored
hashes; `rollback-*.log` retains the outputs. Mutation is a disposable experiment,
not TDD Red history. Ledgers, approvals and historical records were not restored
or truncated. To roll back this task's new evidence in a disposable checkout,
omit its new pin/report/addenda; retain the prior repair bytes and history.
